      package config
      
      import (
      	"errors"
      	"fmt"
      	"os"
      	"slices"
      	"strconv"
      	"strings"
      	"time"
      
      	"github.com/joho/godotenv"
      )
      
      type environment struct {
      	lookup func(string) (string, bool)
      	errs   []error
      }
      
      func newEnvironment() *environment {
      	return newEnvironmentWithLookup(os.LookupEnv)
      }
      
      func newEnvironmentWithLookup(lookup func(string) (string, bool)) *environment {
      	return &environment{lookup: lookup}
      }
      
      // LoadEnvironment loads a dotenv file before configuration constructors run.
      // A missing default .env file is allowed because deployed applications commonly
      // receive their environment directly from the operating system.
      func LoadEnvironment(filenames ...string) error {
      	err := godotenv.Load(filenames...)
      	if err == nil || (len(filenames) == 0 && os.IsNotExist(err)) {
      		return nil
      	}
      
      	return fmt.Errorf("config: load environment: %w", err)
      }
      
      func (e *environment) String(key, fallback string) string {
      	value, ok := e.lookup(key)
      	if !ok {
      		return fallback
      	}
      
      	return value
      }
      
      func (e *environment) RequiredString(key string) string {
      	value, ok := e.lookup(key)
      	if !ok || strings.TrimSpace(value) == "" {
      		e.errs = append(e.errs, fmt.Errorf("%s is required", key))
      	}
      
      	return value
      }
      
      func (e *environment) Int(key string, fallback int) int {
      	value, ok := e.lookup(key)
      	if !ok {
      		return fallback
      	}
      	parsed, err := strconv.Atoi(value)
      	if err != nil {
      		e.add(key, "integer", value, err)
      		return fallback
      	}
      
      	return parsed
      }
      
      func (e *environment) Int32(key string, fallback int32) int32 {
      	value, ok := e.lookup(key)
      	if !ok {
      		return fallback
      	}
      	parsed, err := strconv.ParseInt(value, 10, 32)
      	if err != nil {
      		e.add(key, "32-bit integer", value, err)
      		return fallback
      	}
      
      	return int32(parsed)
      }
      
      func (e *environment) Int64(key string, fallback int64) int64 {
      	value, ok := e.lookup(key)
      	if !ok {
      		return fallback
      	}
      	parsed, err := strconv.ParseInt(value, 10, 64)
      	if err != nil {
      		e.add(key, "64-bit integer", value, err)
      		return fallback
      	}
      
      	return parsed
      }
      
      func (e *environment) Bool(key string, fallback bool) bool {
      	value, ok := e.lookup(key)
      	if !ok {
      		return fallback
      	}
      	parsed, err := strconv.ParseBool(value)
      	if err != nil {
      		e.add(key, "boolean", value, err)
      		return fallback
      	}
      
      	return parsed
      }
      
      func (e *environment) Float64(key string, fallback float64) float64 {
      	value, ok := e.lookup(key)
      	if !ok {
      		return fallback
      	}
      	parsed, err := strconv.ParseFloat(value, 64)
      	if err != nil {
      		e.add(key, "number", value, err)
      		return fallback
      	}
      
      	return parsed
      }
      
      func (e *environment) Duration(key string, fallback time.Duration) time.Duration {
      	value, ok := e.lookup(key)
      	if !ok {
      		return fallback
      	}
      	parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(value)
      	if err != nil {
      		e.add(key, "duration", value, err)
      		return fallback
      	}
      
      	return parsed
      }
      
      func (e *environment) Strings(key string, fallback []string) []string {
      	value, ok := e.lookup(key)
      	if !ok {
      		return slices.Clone(fallback)
      	}
      	if value == "" {
      		return []string{}
      	}
      
      	parts := strings.Split(value, ",")
      	out := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
      	for _, part := range parts {
      		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
      		if part != "" {
      			out = append(out, part)
      		}
      	}
      
      	return out
      }
      
      func (e *environment) Err() error {
      	return errors.Join(e.errs...)
      }
      
      func (e *environment) add(key, expected, value string, err error) {
      	e.errs = append(e.errs, fmt.Errorf(
      		"%s must be a valid %s (got %q): %w",
      		key,
      		expected,
      		value,
      		err,
      	))
      }
      package config
      
      import (
      	"errors"
      	"fmt"
      	"net/url"
      
      	"github.com/mbvlabs/andurel/pkg/validation"
      )
      
      const (
      	DefaultEnvironment = "development"
      	DefaultProjectName = "{{.ProjectName}}"
      	DefaultDomain      = "localhost:8080"
      )
      
      // App contains process-independent application identity and URL settings.
      type App struct {
      	Environment string
      	ProjectName string
      	Domain      string
      	Protocol    string
      	BaseURL     string
      }
      
      func NewApp() (App, error) {
      	env := newEnvironment()
      	cfg := App{
      		Environment: env.String("ENVIRONMENT", DefaultEnvironment),
      		ProjectName: env.String("PROJECT_NAME", DefaultProjectName),
      		Domain:      env.String("DOMAIN", DefaultDomain),
      		Protocol:    env.String("PROTOCOL", ""),
      	}
      	cfg.BaseURL = cfg.baseURL()
      
      	if err := errors.Join(env.Err(), cfg.validate()); err != nil {
      		return App{}, fmt.Errorf("config: app: %w", err)
      	}
      
      	return cfg, nil
      }
      
      func (c App) validate() error {
      	b := validation.NewBuilder()
      	b.Required("Environment", c.Environment)
      	b.Required("ProjectName", c.ProjectName)
      	b.Required("Domain", c.Domain)
      	if err := b.Err(); err != nil {
      		return err
      	}
      	parsed, err := url.Parse(c.BaseURL)
      	if err != nil || parsed.Host == "" ||
      		(parsed.Scheme != "http" && parsed.Scheme != "https") {
      		return fmt.Errorf("application base URL must be an HTTP or HTTPS URL")
      	}
      
      	return nil
      }
      
      func (c App) baseURL() string {
      	protocol := c.Protocol
      	if protocol == "" {
      		protocol = "http"
      	}
      
      	return fmt.Sprintf("%s://%s", protocol, c.Domain)
      }
      
      func (c App) IsProduction() bool {
      	return c.Environment == "production"
      }
      package config
      
      import (

      	"github.com/mbvlabs/andurel/pkg/storage"
      )
      
      const (
      	DefaultDatabaseKind           = "postgres"
      	DefaultDatabaseHost           = "127.0.0.1"
      	DefaultDatabasePort           = "5432"
      	DefaultDatabaseName           = "andurel"
      	DefaultDatabaseUser           = "postgres"
      	DefaultDatabasePassword       = "postgres"
      	DefaultDatabaseSSLMode        = "disable"
      	DefaultDatabaseApplicationName = "andurel"
      )
      
      func NewDatabase() (storage.Config, error) {
      	env := newEnvironment()
      	cfg := storage.DefaultConfig()
      	cfg.DatabaseKind = env.String("DB_KIND", DefaultDatabaseKind)
      	cfg.Host = env.String("DB_HOST", DefaultDatabaseHost)
      	cfg.Port = env.String("DB_PORT", DefaultDatabasePort)
      	cfg.Name = env.String("DB_NAME", DefaultDatabaseName)
      	cfg.User = env.String("DB_USER", DefaultDatabaseUser)
      	cfg.Password = env.String("DB_PASSWORD", DefaultDatabasePassword)
      	cfg.SSLMode = env.String("DB_SSL_MODE", DefaultDatabaseSSLMode)
      	cfg.ApplicationName = env.String("DB_APPLICATION_NAME", DefaultDatabaseApplicationName)
      	cfg.ConnectTimeout = env.Duration("DB_CONNECT_TIMEOUT", cfg.ConnectTimeout)
      	cfg.StatementCacheCapacity = env.Int(
      		"DB_STATEMENT_CACHE_CAPACITY",
      		cfg.StatementCacheCapacity,
      	)
      	cfg.DescriptionCacheCapacity = env.Int(
      		"DB_DESCRIPTION_CACHE_CAPACITY",
      		cfg.DescriptionCacheCapacity,
      	)
      	cfg.MaxOpenConnections = env.Int("DB_MAX_OPEN_CONNECTIONS", cfg.MaxOpenConnections)
      	cfg.MaxIdleConnections = env.Int("DB_MAX_IDLE_CONNECTIONS", cfg.MaxIdleConnections)
      	cfg.ConnectionMaxLifetime = env.Duration(
      		"DB_CONNECTION_MAX_LIFETIME",
      		cfg.ConnectionMaxLifetime,
      	)
      	cfg.ConnectionMaxIdleTime = env.Duration(
      		"DB_CONNECTION_MAX_IDLE_TIME",
      		cfg.ConnectionMaxIdleTime,
      	)
      	cfg.OpenTelemetry = env.Bool("DB_OPEN_TELEMETRY", true)
      
      	if err := errors.Join(env.Err(), cfg.Validate()); err != nil {
      		return storage.Config{}, fmt.Errorf("config: database: %w", err)
      	}
      
      	return cfg, nil
      }
      package config
      
      import (
      	"errors"
      	"fmt"
      
      	"github.com/mbvlabs/andurel/pkg/storage"
      	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
      )
      
      type QueueInsert struct {
      	Config storage.QueueConfig
      }
      
      type QueueWorker struct {
      	Config storage.QueueConfig
      }
      
      func NewQueueInsert() (QueueInsert, error) {
      	env := newEnvironment()
      	cfg := storage.DefaultQueueConfig()
      	cfg.MaxAttempts = env.Int("QUEUE_MAX_ATTEMPTS", cfg.MaxAttempts)
      	cfg.Schema = env.String("QUEUE_SCHEMA", cfg.Schema)
      	cfg.Queues = nil
      	if err := errors.Join(env.Err(), cfg.Validate()); err != nil {
      		return QueueInsert{}, fmt.Errorf("config: queue insert: %w", err)
      	}
      
      	return QueueInsert{Config: cfg}, nil
      }
      
      func NewQueueWorker(insert QueueInsert) (QueueWorker, error) {
      	env := newEnvironment()
      	cfg := insert.Config.Clone()
      	maxWorkers := env.Int("QUEUE_MAX_WORKERS", cfg.MaxWorkers)
      	cfg.AdvisoryLockPrefix = env.Int32("QUEUE_ADVISORY_LOCK_PREFIX", cfg.AdvisoryLockPrefix)
      	cfg.CancelledJobRetentionPeriod = env.Duration(
      		"QUEUE_CANCELLED_JOB_RETENTION_PERIOD",
      		cfg.CancelledJobRetentionPeriod,
      	)
      	cfg.CompletedJobRetentionPeriod = env.Duration(
      		"QUEUE_COMPLETED_JOB_RETENTION_PERIOD",
      		cfg.CompletedJobRetentionPeriod,
      	)
      	cfg.DiscardedJobRetentionPeriod = env.Duration(
      		"QUEUE_DISCARDED_JOB_RETENTION_PERIOD",
      		cfg.DiscardedJobRetentionPeriod,
      	)
      	cfg.FetchCooldown = env.Duration("QUEUE_FETCH_COOLDOWN", cfg.FetchCooldown)
      	cfg.PollInterval = env.Duration("QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL", cfg.PollInterval)
      	cfg.ID = env.String("QUEUE_ID", cfg.ID)
      	cfg.JobCleanerTimeout = env.Duration("QUEUE_JOB_CLEANER_TIMEOUT", cfg.JobCleanerTimeout)
      	cfg.JobStuckThreshold = env.Duration("QUEUE_JOB_STUCK_THRESHOLD", cfg.JobStuckThreshold)
      	cfg.JobTimeout = env.Duration("QUEUE_JOB_TIMEOUT", cfg.JobTimeout)
      	cfg.PollOnly = env.Bool("QUEUE_POLL_ONLY", cfg.PollOnly)
      	cfg.ReindexerTimeout = env.Duration("QUEUE_REINDEXER_TIMEOUT", cfg.ReindexerTimeout)
      	cfg.RescueStuckJobsAfter = env.Duration(
      		"QUEUE_RESCUE_STUCK_JOBS_AFTER",
      		cfg.RescueStuckJobsAfter,
      	)
      	cfg.SoftStopTimeout = env.Duration("QUEUE_SOFT_STOP_TIMEOUT", cfg.SoftStopTimeout)
      	cfg.SkipJobKindValidation = env.Bool(
      		"QUEUE_SKIP_JOB_KIND_VALIDATION",
      		cfg.SkipJobKindValidation,
      	)
      	cfg.SkipUnknownJobCheck = env.Bool(
      		"QUEUE_SKIP_UNKNOWN_JOB_CHECK",
      		cfg.SkipUnknownJobCheck,
      	)
      	cfg.MaxWorkers = maxWorkers
      	cfg.Queues = map[string]river.QueueConfig{
      		river.QueueDefault: {MaxWorkers: maxWorkers},
      	}
      	if err := errors.Join(env.Err(), cfg.Validate()); err != nil {
      		return QueueWorker{}, fmt.Errorf("config: queue worker: %w", err)
      	}
      
      	return QueueWorker{Config: cfg}, nil
      }
      package config
      
      import (
      	"errors"
      	"fmt"
      	"strings"
      
      	"github.com/mbvlabs/andurel/pkg/email"
      )
      
      type MailDriver string
      
      const (
      	MailpitDriver MailDriver = "mailpit"
      {{- if hasExtension .Extensions "aws-ses"}}
      	AWSSESDriver  MailDriver = "aws-ses"
      {{- end}}
      
      	DefaultMailpitHost = "0.0.0.0"
      	DefaultMailpitPort = "1025"
      )
      
      type Mail struct {
      	DefaultSenderSignature string
      }
      
      type MailTransport struct {
      	Driver  MailDriver
      	Mailpit                email.MailpitConfig
      {{- if hasExtension .Extensions "aws-ses"}}
      	AWSSES                 AWSSES
      {{- end}}
      }
      
      func NewMail(app App) (Mail, error) {
      	env := newEnvironment()
      	cfg := Mail{
      		DefaultSenderSignature: env.String("DEFAULT_SENDER_SIGNATURE", "noreply@"+app.Domain),
      	}
      	if strings.TrimSpace(cfg.DefaultSenderSignature) == "" {
      		return Mail{}, fmt.Errorf("config: mail: DEFAULT_SENDER_SIGNATURE must not be empty")
      	}
      
      	return cfg, nil
      }
      
      func NewMailTransport(app App) (MailTransport, error) {
      	env := newEnvironment()
      	defaultDriver := MailDriver("")
      	if !app.IsProduction() {
      		defaultDriver = MailpitDriver
      	}
      {{- if hasExtension .Extensions "aws-ses"}}
      	if app.IsProduction() {
      		defaultDriver = AWSSESDriver
      	}
      {{- end}}
      	cfg := MailTransport{
      		Driver: MailDriver(env.String("EMAIL_PROVIDER", string(defaultDriver))),
      	}
      	var driverErr error
      	switch cfg.Driver {
      	case MailpitDriver:
      		cfg.Mailpit = email.MailpitConfig{
      			Host: env.String("MAILPIT_HOST", DefaultMailpitHost),
      			Port: env.String("MAILPIT_PORT", DefaultMailpitPort),
      		}
      		driverErr = cfg.Mailpit.Validate()
      {{- if hasExtension .Extensions "aws-ses"}}
      	case AWSSESDriver:
      		cfg.AWSSES, driverErr = loadAWSSES(env)
      {{- end}}
      	default:
      		driverErr = fmt.Errorf(
      			"choose an installed EMAIL_PROVIDER in config/email.go (got %q)",
      			cfg.Driver,
      		)
      	}
      
      	if err := errors.Join(env.Err(), driverErr); err != nil {
      		return MailTransport{}, fmt.Errorf("config: mail transport: %w", err)
      	}
      
      	return cfg, nil
      }

      INERTIA FRONTEND VERSIONS
      RELEASE READINESS WORKFLOW
      name: Release Readiness
      
      on:
        push:
          branches:
            - master
      
      permissions:
        contents: read
      
      env:
        GO_VERSION: '1.26.5'
        GOLANGCI_LINT_VERSION: 'v2.12.2'
        GOVULNCHECK_VERSION: 'v1.3.0'
        APIDIFF_VERSION: 'v0.0.0-20250620022241-b7579e27df2b'
      
      jobs:
        readiness:
          name: Release Readiness
          runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
          timeout-minutes: 90
          permissions:
            contents: read
      
          steps:
            - name: Check out the exact commit
              uses: actions/checkout@08eba0b27e820071cde6df949e0beb9ba4906955 # v4.3.0
              with:
                ref: ${{ github.sha }}
                fetch-depth: 0
                persist-credentials: false
      
            - name: Set up pinned Go
              uses: actions/setup-go@4a3601121dd01d1626a1e23e37211e3254c1c06c # v6.4.0
              with:
                go-version: ${{ env.GO_VERSION }}
                check-latest: false
                cache: true
                cache-dependency-path: go.sum
      
            - name: Install pinned validation tools
              shell: bash
              run: |
                set -euo pipefail
                go install github.com/a-h/templ/cmd/templ@v0.3.1020
                go install github.com/segmentio/golines@v0.13.0
                go install golang.org/x/tools/cmd/goimports@v0.44.0
                go install github.com/golangci/golangci-lint/v2/cmd/golangci-lint@${GOLANGCI_LINT_VERSION}
                go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@${GOVULNCHECK_VERSION}
                go install golang.org/x/exp/cmd/apidiff@${APIDIFF_VERSION}
      
            - name: Verify module metadata
              shell: bash
              run: |
                set -euo pipefail
                go mod download
                go mod verify
                go mod tidy -diff
      
            - name: Verify formatting and fixes
              shell: bash
              run: |
                set -euo pipefail
                unformatted="$(gofmt -l .)"
                test -z "${unformatted}"
                go fix -diff ./... | tee /tmp/go-fix.diff
                test ! -s /tmp/go-fix.diff
                git diff --exit-code -- .
      
            - name: Run vet
              run: |
                go vet ./...
                ./scripts/vet-standalone-modules.sh
      
            - name: Check public API and CLI contracts
              shell: bash
              run: |
                set -euo pipefail
                ./scripts/check-contracts.sh
                ./scripts/check-api-compat.sh v2.0.0 v2.0.0
                ./scripts/release_tag_policy_test.sh
      
            - name: Run pinned lint
              run: ./scripts/lint.sh
      
            - name: Run pinned vulnerability scan
              run: govulncheck ./...
      
            - name: Run unit and integration tests with race detection and report coverage
              run: ./scripts/coverage.sh
      
            - name: Upload coverage to Codecov
              uses: codecov/codecov-action@e53489f4d376d79066609109e7a95a29eb3740b1 # v7.0.0
              with:
                token: ${{ secrets.CODECOV_TOKEN }}
                files: ./coverage.out
                disable_search: true
                flags: unit
                fail_ci_if_error: true
      
            - name: Run full end-to-end and scaffold suite
              run: go test ./e2e/... -v -timeout 55m
      
            - name: Upload end-to-end failure artifacts
              if: failure()
              uses: actions/upload-artifact@ea165f8d65b6e75b540449e92b4886f43607fa02 # v4.6.2
              with:
                name: release-readiness-e2e-failures-${{ github.sha }}
                path: /tmp/andurel-e2e-*
                if-no-files-found: ignore
                retention-days: 7
      
            - name: Confirm validation did not mutate tracked files
              if: always()
              shell: bash
              run: |
                set -euo pipefail
                git diff --exit-code -- .
                git diff --cached --exit-code -- .
      
        artifact-preflight:
          name: Release artifact preflight
          needs: readiness
          permissions:
            contents: read
            id-token: write
          uses: ./.github/workflows/release-artifact-preflight.yml
          with:
            checkout_ref: ${{ github.sha }}
            release_tag: ${{ format('v0.0.0-readiness.{0}', github.run_id) }}
            artifact_name: ${{ format('readiness-preflight-assets-{0}', github.sha) }}

## Activity

    $ /usr/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '1,130p' layout/templates/controllers_api.tmpl && sed -n '1,160p' layout/templates/models_model.tmpl && sed -n '1,330p' layout/templates/models_user.tmpl | tail -280 && sed -n '1,130p' layout/templates/database_migrations.tmpl && sed -n '1,200p' layout/templates/database_seeds_seeds.tmpl && sed -n '1,230p' layout/templates/cmd_app_main.tmpl"
    status: Completed · exit 0
      package api
      
      import (
      	"errors"
      	"net/http"
      
      	"{{.ModuleName}}/router"
      	"{{.ModuleName}}/router/routes"
      
      	"github.com/labstack/echo/v5"
      )
      
      type API struct{}
      
      func NewAPI() API {
      	return API{}
      }
      
      func (a API) RegisterRoutes(r *router.Router) error {
      	errs := []error{}
      
      	_, err := r.AddRoute(echo.Route{
      		Method:  http.MethodGet,
      		Path:    routes.Health.Path(),
      		Name:    routes.Health.Name(),
      		Handler: a.Health,
      	})
      	if err != nil {
      		errs = append(errs, err)
      	}
      
      	return errors.Join(errs...)
      }
      
      func (a API) Health(etx *echo.Context) error {
      	return etx.JSON(http.StatusOK, "app is healthy and running")
      }
      // Package models contains data models and validation logic.
      package models
      
      import (
      	"github.com/mbvlabs/andurel/pkg/storage"
      
      	"go.uber.org/fx"
      )
      
      // queryDB is satisfied by storage.Connection and storage.Transaction.
      type queryDB interface {
      	Executor() storage.Executor
      }
      
      var Module = fx.Module(
      	"models",
      	fx.Provide(
      		NewUsers,
      		NewTokens,
      	),
      )
      
      	return b.Err()
      }
      
      func (u *User) HasValidatedEmail() bool {
      	return u.EmailValidatedAt.Valid
      }
      
      func (u *User) ValidPassword(providedPassword, pepper string) (bool, error) {
      	parts := strings.Split(string(u.Password), ":")
      	if len(parts) != 2 {
      		return false, fmt.Errorf("invalid stored password format")
      	}
      
      	expectedHash, err := base64.RawStdEncoding.DecodeString(parts[0])
      	if err != nil {
      		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to decode hash: %w", err)
      	}
      
      	salt, err := base64.RawStdEncoding.DecodeString(parts[1])
      	if err != nil {
      		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to decode pepper: %w", err)
      	}
      
      	newHash := argon2.IDKey(
      		[]byte(providedPassword+pepper),
      		salt,
      		2,
      		19*1024,
      		1,
      		uint32(len(expectedHash)),
      	)
      
      	return subtle.ConstantTimeCompare(newHash, expectedHash) == 1, nil
      }
      
      func (u Users) Find(ctx context.Context, id uuid.UUID) (User, error) {
      	var entity User
      	err := u.db.Executor().NewSelect().
      		Model(&entity).
      		Where("id = ?", id).
      		Scan(ctx)
      	if err != nil {
      		if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
      			return User{}, ErrNotFound
      		}
      
      		return User{}, err
      	}
      
      	return entity, nil
      }
      
      func (u Users) FindByEmail(
      	ctx context.Context,
      	email string,
      ) (User, error) {
      	var entity User
      	err := u.db.Executor().NewSelect().
      		Model(&entity).
      		Where("email = ?", strings.ToLower(email)).
      		Scan(ctx)
      	if err != nil {
      		if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
      			return User{}, ErrNotFound
      		}
      
      		return User{}, err
      	}
      
      	return entity, nil
      }
      
      type PasswordPair struct {
      	Password        string
      	ConfirmPassword string
      }
      
      type CreateUserData struct {
      	Email        string
      	PasswordPair PasswordPair
      }
      
      func (u Users) Create(
      	ctx context.Context,
      	pepper string,
      	data CreateUserData,
      ) (User, error) {
      	hashedPassword, err := HashPassword(data.PasswordPair.Password, pepper)
      	if err != nil {
      		return User{}, err
      	}
      
      	entity := User{
      		ID:               uuid.New(),
      		CreatedAt:        time.Now(),
      		UpdatedAt:        time.Now(),
      		Email:            strings.ToLower(data.Email),
      		EmailValidatedAt: sql.NullTime{},
      		Password:         []byte(hashedPassword),
      		IsAdmin:          false,
      	}
      
      	if err := validation.Validate(&entity); err != nil {
      		return User{}, errors.Join(ErrDomainValidation, err)
      	}
      
      	_, err = u.db.Executor().NewInsert().Model(&entity).Exec(ctx)
      	if err != nil {
      		return User{}, err
      	}
      
      	return entity, nil
      }
      
      type UpdateUserData struct {
      	ID               uuid.UUID
      	Email            string
      	EmailValidatedAt sql.NullTime
      	Password         []byte
      	IsAdmin          bool
      }
      
      func (u Users) Update(
      	ctx context.Context,
      	data UpdateUserData,
      ) (User, error) {
      	var current User
      	err := u.db.Executor().NewSelect().
      		Model(&current).
      		Where("id = ?", data.ID).
      		Scan(ctx)
      	if err != nil {
      		if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
      			return User{}, ErrNotFound
      		}
      
      		return User{}, err
      	}
      
      	email := strings.ToLower(data.Email)
      	if email == "" {
      		email = current.Email
      	}
      
      	emailValidatedAt := data.EmailValidatedAt
      	if !emailValidatedAt.Valid && current.EmailValidatedAt.Valid {
      		emailValidatedAt = current.EmailValidatedAt
      	}
      
      	password := data.Password
      	if len(password) == 0 {
      		password = current.Password
      	}
      
      	entity := User{
      		ID:               data.ID,
      		CreatedAt:        current.CreatedAt,
      		UpdatedAt:        time.Now(),
      		Email:            email,
      		EmailValidatedAt: emailValidatedAt,
      		Password:         password,
      		IsAdmin:          data.IsAdmin,
      	}
      
      	if err := validation.Validate(&entity); err != nil {
      		return User{}, errors.Join(ErrDomainValidation, err)
      	}
      
      	err = u.db.Executor().NewUpdate().
      		Model(&entity).
      		Column("email").
      		Column("email_validated_at").
      		Column("password").
      		Column("is_admin").
      		Column("updated_at").
      		WherePK().
      		Returning("*").
      		Scan(ctx)
      	if err != nil {
      		if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
      			return User{}, ErrNotFound
      		}
      
      		return User{}, err
      	}
      
      	return entity, nil
      }
      
      func (u Users) Destroy(ctx context.Context, id uuid.UUID) error {
      	_, err := u.db.Executor().NewDelete().
      		Model((*User)(nil)).
      		Where("id = ?", id).
      		Exec(ctx)
      
      	return err
      }
      
      func (u Users) All(ctx context.Context) ([]User, error) {
      	var entities []User
      	err := u.db.Executor().NewSelect().
      		Model(&entities).
      		Scan(ctx)
      	if err != nil {
      		return nil, err
      	}
      
      	return entities, nil
      }
      
      type PaginatedUsers struct {
      	Users      []User
      	TotalCount int64
      	Page       int64
      	PageSize   int64
      	TotalPages int64
      }
      
      func (u Users) Paginate(
      	ctx context.Context,
      	page, pageSize int64,
      ) (PaginatedUsers, error) {
      	if page < 1 {
      		page = 1
      	}
      	if pageSize < 1 {
      		pageSize = 10
      	}
      	if pageSize > 100 {
      		pageSize = 100
      	}
      
      	offset := (page - 1) * pageSize
      
      	totalCount, err := u.db.Executor().NewSelect().
      		Model(&User{}).Count(ctx)
      	if err != nil {
      		return PaginatedUsers{}, err
      	}
      
      	entities := make([]User, 0, int(pageSize))
      	err = u.db.Executor().NewSelect().
      		Model(&entities).
      		Limit(int(pageSize)).
      		Offset(int(offset)).
      		Scan(ctx)
      	if err != nil {
      		return PaginatedUsers{}, err
      	}
      
      	totalPages := (int64(totalCount) + pageSize - 1) / pageSize
      
      	return PaginatedUsers{
      		Users:      entities,
      		TotalCount: int64(totalCount),
      		Page:       page,
      		PageSize:   pageSize,
      		TotalPages: totalPages,
      	}, nil
      }
      
      func generateSalt(size int) ([]byte, error) {
      	salt := make([]byte, size)
      	_, err := rand.Read(salt)
      	if err != nil {
      		return nil, err
      	}
      
      	return salt, nil
      }

      		[]byte(password+pepper),
      // Package migrations provides embedded application database migrations.
      package migrations

      //go:embed *.sql
      var Migrations embed.FS
      package seeds

      const Default = "development"
      
      type Runner func(context.Context, storage.Executor) error
      
      var Registry = map[string]Runner{
      	"default":     Development,
      	"development": Development,
      	"test":        Test,
      }
      
      func Names() []string {
      	names := make([]string, 0, len(Registry))
      	for name := range Registry {
      		names = append(names, name)
      	}
      	slices.Sort(names)
      	return names
      }
      
      func Run(ctx context.Context, exec storage.Executor, name string) error {
      	if name == "" {
      		name = Default
      	}
      
      	runner, ok := Registry[name]
      	if !ok {
      		return fmt.Errorf("unknown seed %q (available: %s)", name, strings.Join(Names(), ", "))
      	}
      
      	return runner(ctx, exec)
      }

      var appVersion string
      
      func main() {
      	if err := config.LoadEnvironment(); err != nil {
      		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, err)
      		os.Exit(1)
      	}
      
      	ctx, cancel := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM)
      	defer cancel()
      	app := fx.New(
      		fx.Provide(
      			func() context.Context { return ctx },
      			{{- range .Blueprint.Main.ServiceProvides}}
      			{{.}},
      			{{- end}}
      			newEmailSenders,
      		),
      
      		config.Module,
      		databaseModule,
      		queueInsertModule,
      		models.Module,
      		{{- if .Inertia}}
      		inertiaModule,
      		{{- end}}
      		telemetry.Module,
      		services.Module,
      		controllers.Module,
      		router.Module,
      
      		fx.Invoke(startServer),
      	)
      
      	if err := app.Start(ctx); err != nil {
      		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "%s\n", err)
      		os.Exit(1)
      	}
      
      	<-ctx.Done()
      
      	shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
      	defer cancel()
      
      	if err := app.Stop(shutdownCtx); err != nil {
      		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "%s\n", err)
      		os.Exit(1)
      	}
      }
      
      var databaseModule = fx.Module(

      	fx.Provide(fx.Annotate(newDatabase, fx.As(new(storage.Connection)), fx.As(fx.Self()))),
      )

      )

      func startServer(
      	lc fx.Lifecycle,
      	appCtx context.Context,
      	r *router.Router,
      	appCfg config.App,
      	httpCfg config.HTTP,
      ) {
      	srv := server.New(
      		appCtx,
      		httpCfg.Host,
      		httpCfg.Port,
      		appCfg.Environment,
      		r.Handler,
      		nil,
      		server.WithTimeouts(
      			httpCfg.IdleTimeout,
      			httpCfg.ReadTimeout,
      			httpCfg.WriteTimeout,
      		),
      	)
      	var done <-chan struct{}
      
      	lc.Append(fx.Hook{
      		OnStart: func(_ context.Context) error {
      			slog.InfoContext(
      				appCtx,
      				"starting server",
      				"host",
      				httpCfg.Host,
      				"port",
      				httpCfg.Port,
      			)
      			done = startInBackground(appCtx, "server", func(ctx context.Context) error {
      				return srv.Start(ctx, appCfg.Environment)
      			})
      			return nil
      		},
      		OnStop: func(ctx context.Context) error {
      			slog.InfoContext(ctx, "initiating graceful shutdown")
      			return stopAndWait(ctx, func(ctx context.Context) error {
      				var shutdownErr error
      				for _, shutdowner := range srv.Shutdowners {
      					if err := shutdowner.Shutdown(ctx); err != nil {
      						shutdownErr = errors.Join(
      							shutdownErr,
      							fmt.Errorf("server: shutdown component %T: %w", shutdowner, err),
      						)
      					}
      				}
      
      				return shutdownErr
      			}, done)
      		},
      	})
      }
      
      func newDatabase(
      	lifecycle fx.Lifecycle,
      	ctx context.Context,
      	cfg storage.Config,
      ) (*storage.Postgres, error) {
      	db, err := storage.NewPostgres(ctx, cfg)
      	if err != nil {
      		return nil, err
      	}
      
      	lifecycle.Append(fx.Hook{OnStop: func(context.Context) error { return db.Close() }})
      	return db, nil
      }
      
      func newQueueInsert(
      	connection storage.Connection,
      	cfg config.QueueInsert,
      ) (*storage.QueueInsert, error) {
      	return storage.NewQueueInsert(connection, cfg.Config)
      }
      
      {{if .Inertia}}
      
      func newInertia(
      	lifecycle fx.Lifecycle,
      	appCfg config.App,
      	cfg config.Inertia,
      ) (*inertia.Renderer, error) {
      	renderer, err := inertia.New(
      		inertia.WithRoot(views.Root),
      		inertia.WithAssetFS(assets.Files),
      		inertia.WithProjectName(appCfg.ProjectName),
      		inertia.WithEnvironment(appCfg.Environment),
      		inertia.WithBuildPathURL(routes.ViteBuild.Path()),
      		inertia.WithEntryPoint(cfg.EntryPoint),
      		inertia.WithContainerID(cfg.ContainerID),
      		inertia.WithViteDevURL(cfg.ViteDevURL),
      		inertia.WithProtocolDebug(cfg.ProtocolDebug),
      		inertia.WithShared(inertia.Props{"appVersion": appVersion}),
      		inertia.WithSSRMode(cfg.SSRMode),
      		inertia.WithSSRRuntime(cfg.SSRRuntime),
      		inertia.WithSSRBundle(cfg.SSRBundle),
      		inertia.WithSSRURL(cfg.SSRURL),
      		inertia.WithSSRStartupTimeout(cfg.SSRStartupTimeout),
      		inertia.WithSSRRequestTimeout(cfg.SSRRequestTimeout),
      		inertia.WithSSRMaxResponseBytes(cfg.SSRMaxResponseBytes),
      		inertia.WithSSRFailFast(cfg.SSRFailFast),
      	)
      	if err != nil {
      		return nil, err
      	}
      
      	lifecycle.Append(fx.Hook{
      		OnStart: renderer.Start,
      		OnStop:  renderer.Shutdown,
      	})
      	return renderer, nil
      }

      #!/usr/bin/env bash
      set -euo pipefail

          23		cfg := storage.DefaultConfig()
          24		cfg.DatabaseKind = env.String("DB_KIND", DefaultDatabaseKind)
          25		cfg.Host = env.String("DB_HOST", DefaultDatabaseHost)
          26		cfg.Port = env.String("DB_PORT", DefaultDatabasePort)
          27		cfg.Name = env.String("DB_NAME", DefaultDatabaseName)
          28		cfg.User = env.String("DB_USER", DefaultDatabaseUser)
          29		cfg.Password = env.String("DB_PASSWORD", DefaultDatabasePassword)
          30		cfg.SSLMode = env.String("DB_SSL_MODE", DefaultDatabaseSSLMode)
          31		cfg.ApplicationName = env.String("DB_APPLICATION_NAME", DefaultDatabaseApplicationName)
          32		cfg.ConnectTimeout = env.Duration("DB_CONNECT_TIMEOUT", cfg.ConnectTimeout)
          33		cfg.StatementCacheCapacity = env.Int(
          34			"DB_STATEMENT_CACHE_CAPACITY",
          35			cfg.StatementCacheCapacity,
          36		)
          37		cfg.DescriptionCacheCapacity = env.Int(
          38			"DB_DESCRIPTION_CACHE_CAPACITY",
          39			cfg.DescriptionCacheCapacity,
          40		)
          41		cfg.MaxOpenConnections = env.Int("DB_MAX_OPEN_CONNECTIONS", cfg.MaxOpenConnections)
          42		cfg.MaxIdleConnections = env.Int("DB_MAX_IDLE_CONNECTIONS", cfg.MaxIdleConnections)
          43		cfg.ConnectionMaxLifetime = env.Duration(
          44			"DB_CONNECTION_MAX_LIFETIME",
          45			cfg.ConnectionMaxLifetime,
          46		)
          47		cfg.ConnectionMaxIdleTime = env.Duration(
          48			"DB_CONNECTION_MAX_IDLE_TIME",
          49			cfg.ConnectionMaxIdleTime,
          50		)
          51		cfg.OpenTelemetry = env.Bool("DB_OPEN_TELEMETRY", true)
          52
          53		if err := errors.Join(env.Err(), cfg.Validate()); err != nil {
          54			return storage.Config{}, fmt.Errorf("config: database: %w", err)
          55		}
          56
          57		return cfg, nil
          58	}

          36		"github.com/mbvlabs/andurel/pkg/server"
          37		"github.com/mbvlabs/andurel/pkg/storage"
          38		"go.uber.org/fx"
          39	)
          40
          41	var appVersion string
          42
          43	func main() {
          44		if err := config.LoadEnvironment(); err != nil {
          45			fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, err)
          46			os.Exit(1)
          47		}
          48
          49		ctx, cancel := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM)
          50		defer cancel()
          51		app := fx.New(
          52			fx.Provide(
          53				func() context.Context { return ctx },
          54				{{- range .Blueprint.Main.ServiceProvides}}
          55				{{.}},
          56				{{- end}}
          57				newEmailSenders,
          58			),
          59
          60			config.Module,
          61			databaseModule,
          62			queueInsertModule,
          63			models.Module,
          64			{{- if .Inertia}}
          65			inertiaModule,
          66			{{- end}}
          67			telemetry.Module,
          68			services.Module,
          69			controllers.Module,
          70			router.Module,
          71
          72			fx.Invoke(startServer),
          73		)
          74
          75		if err := app.Start(ctx); err != nil {
          76			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "%s\n", err)
          77			os.Exit(1)
          78		}
          79
          80		<-ctx.Done()
          81
          82		shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
          83		defer cancel()
          84
          85		if err := app.Stop(shutdownCtx); err != nil {
          86			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "%s\n", err)
          87			os.Exit(1)
          88		}
          89	}
          90
          91	var databaseModule = fx.Module(
          92		"database",
          93		fx.Provide(fx.Annotate(newDatabase, fx.As(new(storage.Connection)), fx.As(fx.Self()))),
          94	)
          95
          96	var queueInsertModule = fx.Module(
          97		"queue-insert",
          98		fx.Provide(fx.Annotate(newQueueInsert, fx.As(new(storage.InsertQueue)))),
          99	)
         100
         101	{{- if .Inertia}}
         102	var inertiaModule = fx.Module(
         103		"inertia",
         104		fx.Provide(newInertia),
         105	)
         106
         107	{{- end}}
         108
         109	func startServer(
         110		lc fx.Lifecycle,
         111		appCtx context.Context,
         112		r *router.Router,
         113		appCfg config.App,
         114		httpCfg config.HTTP,
         115	) {
         116		srv := server.New(
         117			appCtx,
         118			httpCfg.Host,
         119			httpCfg.Port,
         120			appCfg.Environment,
         121			r.Handler,
         122			nil,
         123			server.WithTimeouts(
         124				httpCfg.IdleTimeout,
         125				httpCfg.ReadTimeout,
         126				httpCfg.WriteTimeout,
         127			),
         128		)
         129		var done <-chan struct{}
         130
         131		lc.Append(fx.Hook{
         132			OnStart: func(_ context.Context) error {
         133				slog.InfoContext(
         134					appCtx,
         135					"starting server",
         136					"host",
         137					httpCfg.Host,
         138					"port",
         139					httpCfg.Port,
         140				)
         141				done = startInBackground(appCtx, "server", func(ctx context.Context) error {
         142					return srv.Start(ctx, appCfg.Environment)
         143				})
         144				return nil
         145			},
         146			OnStop: func(ctx context.Context) error {
         147				slog.InfoContext(ctx, "initiating graceful shutdown")
         148				return stopAndWait(ctx, func(ctx context.Context) error {
         149					var shutdownErr error
         150					for _, shutdowner := range srv.Shutdowners {
         151						if err := shutdowner.Shutdown(ctx); err != nil {
         152							shutdownErr = errors.Join(
         153								shutdownErr,
         154								fmt.Errorf("server: shutdown component %T: %w", shutdowner, err),
         155							)
         156						}
         157					}
         158
         159					return shutdownErr
         160				}, done)
         161			},
         162		})
         163	}
         164
         165	func newDatabase(
         166		lifecycle fx.Lifecycle,
         167		ctx context.Context,
         168		cfg storage.Config,
         169	) (*storage.Postgres, error) {
         170		db, err := storage.NewPostgres(ctx, cfg)
         171		if err != nil {
         172			return nil, err
         173		}
         174
         175		lifecycle.Append(fx.Hook{OnStop: func(context.Context) error { return db.Close() }})
         176		return db, nil
         177	}
         178
         179	func newQueueInsert(
         180		connection storage.Connection,
         181		cfg config.QueueInsert,
         182	) (*storage.QueueInsert, error) {
         183		return storage.NewQueueInsert(connection, cfg.Config)
         184	}
         185
         186	{{if .Inertia}}
         187
         188	func newInertia(
         189		lifecycle fx.Lifecycle,
         190		appCfg config.App,
         191		cfg config.Inertia,
         192	) (*inertia.Renderer, error) {
         193		renderer, err := inertia.New(
         194			inertia.WithRoot(views.Root),
         195			inertia.WithAssetFS(assets.Files),
         196			inertia.WithProjectName(appCfg.ProjectName),
         197			inertia.WithEnvironment(appCfg.Environment),
         198			inertia.WithBuildPathURL(routes.ViteBuild.Path()),
         199			inertia.WithEntryPoint(cfg.EntryPoint),
         200			inertia.WithContainerID(cfg.ContainerID),
         201			inertia.WithViteDevURL(cfg.ViteDevURL),
         202			inertia.WithProtocolDebug(cfg.ProtocolDebug),
         203			inertia.WithShared(inertia.Props{"appVersion": appVersion}),
         204			inertia.WithSSRMode(cfg.SSRMode),
         205			inertia.WithSSRRuntime(cfg.SSRRuntime),
         206			inertia.WithSSRBundle(cfg.SSRBundle),
         207			inertia.WithSSRURL(cfg.SSRURL),
         208			inertia.WithSSRStartupTimeout(cfg.SSRStartupTimeout),
         209			inertia.WithSSRRequestTimeout(cfg.SSRRequestTimeout),
         210			inertia.WithSSRMaxResponseBytes(cfg.SSRMaxResponseBytes),
         211			inertia.WithSSRFailFast(cfg.SSRFailFast),
         212		)
         213		if err != nil {
         214			return nil, err
         215		}
         216
         217		lifecycle.Append(fx.Hook{
         218			OnStart: renderer.Start,
         219			OnStop:  renderer.Shutdown,
         220		})
         221		return renderer, nil
         222	}

          20		"github.com/testcontainers/testcontainers-go/wait"
          21		"github.com/uptrace/bun"
          22		"github.com/uptrace/bun/dialect/pgdialect"
          23	)
          24
          25	// Executor is the query interface satisfied by *bun.DB, *bun.Tx, and *bun.Conn.
          26	// It remains available for application-owned model and factory APIs.
          27	type Executor = bun.IDB
          28
          29	// Connection exposes the Bun executor and its underlying database/sql pool.
          30	type Connection interface {
          31		Executor() bun.IDB
          32		DB() *sql.DB
          33		BeginTransaction(ctx context.Context, opts *sql.TxOptions) (Transaction, error)
          34	}
          35
          36	// Postgres wraps a bun.DB.
          37	type Postgres struct {
          38		bun *bun.DB
          39	}
          40
          41	var _ Connection = (*Postgres)(nil)
          42
          43	// NewPostgres creates a new database connection from the supplied configuration.
          44	func NewPostgres(ctx context.Context, config Config, options ...Option) (*Postgres, error) {
          45		settings := postgresOptions{
          46			config:            config,
          47			runtimeParameters: make(map[string]string),
          48		}
          49		for _, option := range options {
          50			if option == nil {
          51				continue
          52			}
          53			if err := option(&settings); err != nil {
          54				return nil, fmt.Errorf("storage: configure postgres: %w", err)
          55			}
          56		}
          57
          58		if !settings.telemetrySet && settings.config.OpenTelemetry {
          59			settings.telemetry = &TelemetryConfig{}
          60		}
          61		if settings.connectTimeout != nil {
          62			settings.config.ConnectTimeout = *settings.connectTimeout
          63		}
          64		if settings.statementCacheCapacity != nil {
          65			settings.config.StatementCacheCapacity = *settings.statementCacheCapacity
          66		}
          67		if settings.descriptionCacheCapacity != nil {
          68			settings.config.DescriptionCacheCapacity = *settings.descriptionCacheCapacity
          69		}
          70		if settings.maxOpenConnections != nil {
          71			settings.config.MaxOpenConnections = *settings.maxOpenConnections
          72		}
          73		if settings.maxIdleConnections != nil {
          74			settings.config.MaxIdleConnections = *settings.maxIdleConnections
          75		}
          76		if settings.connectionMaxLifetime != nil {
          77			settings.config.ConnectionMaxLifetime = *settings.connectionMaxLifetime
          78		}
          79		if settings.connectionMaxIdleTime != nil {
          80			settings.config.ConnectionMaxIdleTime = *settings.connectionMaxIdleTime
          81		}
          82
          83		databaseURL := settings.configURL
          84		if databaseURL == "" {
          85			var err error
          86			databaseURL, err = settings.config.DatabaseURL()
          87			if err != nil {
          88				return nil, fmt.Errorf("storage: configure postgres: %w", err)
          89			}
          90		}
          91		pgxConfig, err := pgx.ParseConfig(databaseURL)
          92		if err != nil {
          93			return nil, fmt.Errorf("storage: parse database URL: %w", err)
          94		}
          95		if settings.telemetry != nil {
          96			pgxConfig.Tracer = otelpgx.NewTracer(telemetryOptions(*settings.telemetry)...)
          97		}
          98		// URL settings retain pgx's parsed defaults and URL query parameters.
          99		// Explicit field options take precedence for either configuration source.
         100		if settings.configURL == "" {
         101			pgxConfig.ConnectTimeout = settings.config.ConnectTimeout
         102			pgxConfig.StatementCacheCapacity = settings.config.StatementCacheCapacity
         103			pgxConfig.DescriptionCacheCapacity = settings.config.DescriptionCacheCapacity
         104			if settings.config.ApplicationName != "" {
         105				pgxConfig.RuntimeParams["application_name"] = settings.config.ApplicationName
         106			}
         107		}
         108		if settings.connectTimeout != nil {
         109			pgxConfig.ConnectTimeout = *settings.connectTimeout
         110		}
         111		if settings.applicationName != nil {
         112			pgxConfig.RuntimeParams["application_name"] = *settings.applicationName
         113		}
         114		if settings.statementCacheCapacity != nil {
         115			pgxConfig.StatementCacheCapacity = *settings.statementCacheCapacity
         116		}
         117		if settings.descriptionCacheCapacity != nil {
         118			pgxConfig.DescriptionCacheCapacity = *settings.descriptionCacheCapacity
         119		}
         120		if pgxConfig.StatementCacheCapacity == 0 && pgxConfig.DefaultQueryExecMode == pgx.QueryExecModeCacheStatement {
         121			pgxConfig.DefaultQueryExecMode = pgx.QueryExecModeExec
         122		}
         123		if pgxConfig.DescriptionCacheCapacity == 0 && pgxConfig.DefaultQueryExecMode == pgx.QueryExecModeCacheDescribe {
         124			pgxConfig.DefaultQueryExecMode = pgx.QueryExecModeDescribeExec
         125		}
         126		maps.Copy(pgxConfig.RuntimeParams, settings.runtimeParameters)
         127		if settings.tlsConfig != nil {
         128			pgxConfig.TLSConfig = settings.tlsConfig.Clone()
         129			for i := range pgxConfig.Fallbacks {
         130				pgxConfig.Fallbacks[i].TLSConfig = settings.tlsConfig.Clone()
         131			}
         132		}
         133
         134		sqldb := stdlib.OpenDB(*pgxConfig)
         135		maxOpenConnections := settings.config.MaxOpenConnections
         136		if settings.maxOpenConnections != nil {
         137			maxOpenConnections = *settings.maxOpenConnections
         138		}
         139		sqldb.SetMaxOpenConns(maxOpenConnections)
         140		maxIdleConnections := settings.config.MaxIdleConnections
         141		if settings.maxIdleConnections != nil {
         142			maxIdleConnections = *settings.maxIdleConnections
         143		}
         144		sqldb.SetMaxIdleConns(maxIdleConnections)
         145		connectionMaxLifetime := settings.config.ConnectionMaxLifetime
         146		if settings.connectionMaxLifetime != nil {
         147			connectionMaxLifetime = *settings.connectionMaxLifetime
         148		}
         149		sqldb.SetConnMaxLifetime(connectionMaxLifetime)
         150		connectionMaxIdleTime := settings.config.ConnectionMaxIdleTime
         151		if settings.connectionMaxIdleTime != nil {
         152			connectionMaxIdleTime = *settings.connectionMaxIdleTime
         153		}
         154		sqldb.SetConnMaxIdleTime(connectionMaxIdleTime)
         155		db := bun.NewDB(sqldb, pgdialect.New())
         156
         157		if err := db.PingContext(ctx); err != nil {
         158			db.Close()
         159			return nil, fmt.Errorf("storage: ping database: %w", err)
         160		}
         161
         162		return &Postgres{bun: db}, nil
         163	}
         164
         165	// Executor returns the Bun query executor.
         166	func (p *Postgres) Executor() bun.IDB {
         167		return p.bun
         168	}
         169
         170	// DB returns the underlying sql.DB.
         171	func (p *Postgres) DB() *sql.DB {
         172		return p.bun.DB
         173	}
         174
         175	// Close closes the database connection.
         176	func (p *Postgres) Close() error {
         177		return p.bun.Close()
         178	}
         179
         180	// BeginTransaction starts a transaction with Bun and database/sql access.
         181	func (p *Postgres) BeginTransaction(
         182		ctx context.Context,
         183		opts *sql.TxOptions,
         184	) (Transaction, error) {
         185		tx, err := p.bun.BeginTx(ctx, opts)
         186		if err != nil {
         187			return nil, fmt.Errorf("storage: begin transaction: %w", err)
         188		}
         189
         190		return bunTransaction{tx: tx}, nil
         191	}
         192
         193	// TestCluster owns a Postgres test container and can create isolated databases.
         194	type TestCluster struct {
         195		container *postgres.PostgresContainer
         196		host      string
         197		port      string
         198		user      string
         199		password  string
         200		adminDB   string
         201	}
         202
         203	// NewTestCluster starts a Postgres container for a test package.
         204	func NewTestCluster(ctx context.Context) (*TestCluster, error) {
         205		const (
           1	package storage
           2
           3	import (
           4		_ "embed"
           5		"fmt"
           6		"os"
           7		"path/filepath"
           8		"strings"
           9	)
          10
          11	const (
          12		// SQLCConfigFile is the project-root sqlc configuration filename.
          13		SQLCConfigFile = "sqlc.yaml"
          14		// SQLCQueriesDir holds hand-written SQL query files for sqlc.
          15		SQLCQueriesDir = "models/queries"
          16		// SQLCGeneratedDir is the sqlc output directory beneath models/internal.
          17		SQLCGeneratedDir = "models/internal/queries"
          18	)
          19
          20	//go:embed sqlc.yaml
          21	var sqlcConfig []byte
          22
          23	// SQLCConfig returns the canonical Andurel sqlc configuration.
          24	func SQLCConfig() []byte {
          25		out := make([]byte, len(sqlcConfig))
          26		copy(out, sqlcConfig)
          27		return out
          28	}
          29
          30	// WriteSQLCConfig writes the canonical sqlc.yaml into dir.
          31	func WriteSQLCConfig(dir string) error {
          32		if strings.TrimSpace(dir) == "" {
          33			return fmt.Errorf("storage: sqlc config directory is required")
          34		}
          35
          36		path := filepath.Join(dir, SQLCConfigFile)
          37		if err := os.WriteFile(path, sqlcConfig, 0o644); err != nil {
          38			return fmt.Errorf("storage: write sqlc config: %w", err)
          39		}
          40
          41		return nil
          42	}
          43
          44	// HasSQLCQueryFiles reports whether models/queries contains sqlc query
          45	// definitions. Files must include at least one sqlc name annotation.
          46	func HasSQLCQueryFiles(projectRoot string) (bool, error) {
          47		queriesDir := filepath.Join(projectRoot, SQLCQueriesDir)
          48		entries, err := os.ReadDir(queriesDir)
          49		if err != nil {
          50			if os.IsNotExist(err) {
          51				return false, nil
          52			}
          53			return false, fmt.Errorf("storage: read sqlc queries directory: %w", err)
          54		}
          55
          56		for _, entry := range entries {
          57			if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".sql") {
          58				continue
          59			}
          60
          61			content, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(queriesDir, entry.Name()))
          62			if err != nil {
          63				return false, fmt.Errorf("storage: read sqlc query file %s: %w", entry.Name(), err)
          64			}
          65			if containsSQLCQueryAnnotation(string(content)) {
          66				return true, nil
          67			}
          68		}
          69
          70		return false, nil
          71	}
          72
          73	func containsSQLCQueryAnnotation(content string) bool {
          74		return strings.Contains(content, "-- name:")
          75	}

          31	// DatabaseConfig records database generation settings.

          36	// ScaffoldConfig records the options used to create a project.
          37	type ScaffoldConfig struct {
          38		ProjectName              string `json:"projectName"`
          39		Database                 string `json:"database"`
          40		Inertia                  string `json:"inertia,omitempty"`
          41		JavaScriptPackageManager string `json:"javascriptPackageManager,omitempty"`
          42		InertiaSSRRuntime        string `json:"inertiaSSRRuntime,omitempty"`
          43		JavaScriptRuntime        string `json:"javascriptRuntime,omitempty"` // Deprecated: use JavaScriptPackageManager.
          44	}
          45
          46	// PackageManager returns the configured JavaScript package manager. Locks from
          47	// before V2 used javascriptRuntime for this value.
          48	func (config *ScaffoldConfig) PackageManager() string {
          49		if config == nil {
          50			return ""
          51		}
          52		if config.JavaScriptPackageManager != "" {
          53			return config.JavaScriptPackageManager
          54		}
          55		return config.JavaScriptRuntime
          56	}
          57
          58	// SSRRuntime returns the JavaScript executable selected for Inertia SSR.
          59	func (config *ScaffoldConfig) SSRRuntime() string {
          60		if config == nil || config.InertiaSSRRuntime == "" {
          61			return "node"
          62		}
          63		return config.InertiaSSRRuntime
          64	}
          65
          66	// Extension records when an extension was applied.
          67	type Extension struct {
          68		AppliedAt string `json:"appliedAt"`
          69	}
          70
          71	// ToolDownload describes how to download a managed tool binary.
          72	type ToolDownload struct {
           1	# Releasing Andurel
           2
           3	Release tags must identify a commit that has already passed the canonical readiness gate on `master`.
           4
           5	1. Merge the release changes to `master`.
           6	2. Wait for the `Release Readiness` check to pass on that exact `master` commit.
           7	3. Create and push the new version tag only after that check is green.
           8
           9	The tag-triggered release workflow verifies tag identity and confirms that the tagged commit has a successful post-merge `Release Readiness` run on `master`. It does not create artifacts until that proof passes. It then creates a signed private draft, smoke-tests every supported archive, attests the release artifacts and SBOMs, and publishes only the verified draft.
          10
          11	## Standalone modules
          12
          13	Modules under `pkg/` have independent semantic versions. Use Go's subdirectory
          14	tag format, for example `pkg/inertia/v0.1.0`. Before tagging, verify the module
          15	with `GOWORK=off`, update its `CHANGELOG.md`, and update the framework's verified
          16	package version set. Generated applications must resolve the tag without a
          17	workspace or `replace` directive.
           1	name: Release Readiness
           2
           3	on:
           4	  push:
           5	    branches:
           6	      - master
           7
           8	permissions:
           9	  contents: read
          10
          11	env:
          12	  GO_VERSION: '1.26.5'
          13	  GOLANGCI_LINT_VERSION: 'v2.12.2'
          14	  GOVULNCHECK_VERSION: 'v1.3.0'
          15	  APIDIFF_VERSION: 'v0.0.0-20250620022241-b7579e27df2b'
          16
          17	jobs:
          18	  readiness:
          19	    name: Release Readiness
          20	    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
          21	    timeout-minutes: 90
          22	    permissions:
          23	      contents: read
          24
          25	    steps:
          26	      - name: Check out the exact commit
          27	        uses: actions/checkout@08eba0b27e820071cde6df949e0beb9ba4906955 # v4.3.0
          28	        with:
          29	          ref: ${{ github.sha }}
          30	          fetch-depth: 0
          31	          persist-credentials: false
          32
          33	      - name: Set up pinned Go
          34	        uses: actions/setup-go@4a3601121dd01d1626a1e23e37211e3254c1c06c # v6.4.0
          35	        with:
          36	          go-version: ${{ env.GO_VERSION }}
          37	          check-latest: false
          38	          cache: true
          39	          cache-dependency-path: go.sum
          40
          41	      - name: Install pinned validation tools
          42	        shell: bash
          43	        run: |
          44	          set -euo pipefail
          45	          go install github.com/a-h/templ/cmd/templ@v0.3.1020
          46	          go install github.com/segmentio/golines@v0.13.0
          47	          go install golang.org/x/tools/cmd/goimports@v0.44.0
          48	          go install github.com/golangci/golangci-lint/v2/cmd/golangci-lint@${GOLANGCI_LINT_VERSION}
          49	          go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@${GOVULNCHECK_VERSION}
          50	          go install golang.org/x/exp/cmd/apidiff@${APIDIFF_VERSION}
          51
          52	      - name: Verify module metadata
          53	        shell: bash
          54	        run: |
          55	          set -euo pipefail
          56	          go mod download
          57	          go mod verify
          58	          go mod tidy -diff
          59
          60	      - name: Verify formatting and fixes
          61	        shell: bash
          62	        run: |
          63	          set -euo pipefail
          64	          unformatted="$(gofmt -l .)"
          65	          test -z "${unformatted}"
          66	          go fix -diff ./... | tee /tmp/go-fix.diff
          67	          test ! -s /tmp/go-fix.diff
          68	          git diff --exit-code -- .
          69
          70	      - name: Run vet
          71	        run: |
          72	          go vet ./...
          73	          ./scripts/vet-standalone-modules.sh
          74
          75	      - name: Check public API and CLI contracts
          76	        shell: bash
          77	        run: |
          78	          set -euo pipefail
          79	          ./scripts/check-contracts.sh
          80	          ./scripts/check-api-compat.sh v2.0.0 v2.0.0
          81	          ./scripts/release_tag_policy_test.sh
          82
          83	      - name: Run pinned lint
          84	        run: ./scripts/lint.sh
          85
          86	      - name: Run pinned vulnerability scan
          87	        run: govulncheck ./...
          88
          89	      - name: Run unit and integration tests with race detection and report coverage
          90	        run: ./scripts/coverage.sh
          91
          92	      - name: Upload coverage to Codecov
          93	        uses: codecov/codecov-action@e53489f4d376d79066609109e7a95a29eb3740b1 # v7.0.0
          94	        with:
          95	          token: ${{ secrets.CODECOV_TOKEN }}
          96	          files: ./coverage.out
          97	          disable_search: true
          98	          flags: unit
          99	          fail_ci_if_error: true
         100
         101	      - name: Run full end-to-end and scaffold suite
         102	        run: go test ./e2e/... -v -timeout 55m
         103
         104	      - name: Upload end-to-end failure artifacts
         105	        if: failure()
         106	        uses: actions/upload-artifact@ea165f8d65b6e75b540449e92b4886f43607fa02 # v4.6.2
         107	        with:
         108	          name: release-readiness-e2e-failures-${{ github.sha }}
         109	          path: /tmp/andurel-e2e-*
         110	          if-no-files-found: ignore
         111	          retention-days: 7
         112
         113	      - name: Confirm validation did not mutate tracked files
         114	        if: always()
         115	        shell: bash
         116	        run: |
         117	          set -euo pipefail
         118	          git diff --exit-code -- .
         119	          git diff --cached --exit-code -- .
         120
         121	  artifact-preflight:
         122	    name: Release artifact preflight
         123	    needs: readiness
         124	    permissions:
         125	      contents: read
         126	      id-token: write
         127	    uses: ./.github/workflows/release-artifact-preflight.yml
         128	    with:
         129	      checkout_ref: ${{ github.sha }}
         130	      release_tag: ${{ format('v0.0.0-readiness.{0}', github.run_id) }}
